Understanding the lifecycle is essential for effective build configuration and optimization.\n\n## Default Lifecycle Phases\n\n### Complete Phase Order\n\n```\n1.  validate      - Validate project structure\n2.  initialize    - Initialize build state\n3.  generate-sources\n4.  process-sources\n5.  generate-resources\n6.  process-resources - Copy resources to output\n7.  compile       - Compile source code\n8.  process-classes\n9.  generate-test-sources\n10. process-test-sources\n11. generate-test-resources\n12. process-test-resources\n13. test-compile  - Compile test sources\n14. process-test-classes\n15. test          - Run unit tests\n16. prepare-package\n17. package       - Create JAR/WAR\n18. pre-integration-test\n19. integration-test - Run integration tests\n20. post-integration-test\n21. verify        - Run verification checks\n22. install       - Install to local repo\n23. deploy        - Deploy to remote repo\n```\n\n### Common Phase Commands\n\n```bash\n# Compile only\nmvn compile\n\n# Compile and run tests\nmvn test\n\n# Create package\nmvn package\n\n# Install to local repository\nmvn install\n\n# Deploy to remote repository\nmvn deploy\n\n# Clean and build\nmvn clean install\n\n# Skip tests\nmvn install -DskipTests\n\n# Skip test compilation and execution\nmvn install -Dmaven.test.skip=true\n```\n\n## Clean Lifecycle\n\n```\n1. pre-clean\n2. clean         - Delete target directory\n3. post-clean\n```\n\n```bash\n# Clean build artifacts\nmvn clean\n\n# Clean specific directory\nmvn clean -DbuildDirectory=out\n```\n\n## Site Lifecycle\n\n```\n1. pre-site\n2. site          - Generate documentation\n3. post-site\n4. site-deploy   - Deploy documentation\n```\n\n```bash\n# Generate site\nmvn site\n\n# Generate and deploy site\nmvn site-deploy\n```\n\n## Goals vs Phases\n\n### Executing Phases\n\n```bash\n# Execute phase (runs all previous phases)\nmvn package\n```\n\n### Executing Goals\n\n```bash\n# Execute specific goal\nmvn compiler:compile\nmvn surefire:test\nmvn jar:jar\n\n# Multiple goals\nmvn dependency:tree compiler:compile\n```\n\n### Phase-to-Goal Bindings\n\n```xml\n<build>\n    <plugins>\n        <plugin>\n            <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>\n            <artifactId>maven-compiler-plugin</artifactId>\n            <version>3.12.1</version>\n            <executions>\n                <execution>\n                    <id>compile-sources</id>\n                    <phase>compile</phase>\n                    <goals>\n                        <goal>compile</goal>\n                    </goals>\n                </execution>\n            </executions>\n        </plugin>\n    </plugins>\n</build>\n```\n\n## Build Profiles\n\n### Profile Definition\n\n```xml\n<profiles>\n    <profile>\n        <id>development</id>\n        <activation>\n            <activeByDefault>true</activeByDefault>\n        </activation>\n        <properties>\n            <env>dev</env>\n            <skip.integration.tests>true</skip.integration.tests>\n        </properties>\n    </profile>\n\n    <profile>\n        <id>production</id>\n        <properties>\n            <env>prod</env>\n            <skip.integration.tests>false</skip.integration.tests>\n        </properties>\n        <build>\n            <plugins>\n                <plugin>\n                    <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>\n                    <artifactId>maven-compiler-plugin</artifactId>\n                    <configuration>\n                        <debug>false</debug>\n                        <optimize>true</optimize>\n                    </configuration>\n                </plugin>\n            </plugins>\n        </build>\n    </profile>\n</profiles>\n```\n\n### Profile Activation\n\n```bash\n# Activate by name\nmvn install -Pproduction\n\n# Multiple profiles\nmvn install -Pproduction,ci\n\n# Deactivate profile\nmvn install -P!development\n```\n\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

It handles transitive dependencies, version conflicts, and provides mechanisms for controlling dependency resolution across multi-module projects.\n\n## Dependency Declaration\n\n### Basic Dependency\n\n```xml\n<dependency>\n    <groupId>org.springframework.boot</groupId>\n    <artifactId>spring-boot-starter-web</artifactId>\n    <version>3.2.0</version>\n</dependency>\n```\n\n### Dependency with Scope\n\n```xml\n<dependency>\n    <groupId>org.junit.jupiter</groupId>\n    <artifactId>junit-jupiter</artifactId>\n    <version>5.10.1</version>\n This issue stems from the compile-testing version used, which expects tools.jar for JDK8 but is unnecessary in JDK9+. Upgrading compile-testing to a newer version without this dependency or excluding com.sun:tools via dependencyManagement could fix it, though compatibility with older Java versions must be verified."}}
{"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_56","type":"reasoning","text":"**Evaluating strategies for tools.jar dependency override**\n\nConsidering overriding the toolsjar property to a dummy path or disabling the default profile, but systemPath cannot be empty and dummy jars may cause issues. The classes needed exist in JDK 9+ modules like jdk.compiler, which aren’t always resolved by default at runtime, potentially requiring `--add-modules jdk.compiler`. Excluding com.sun:tools via dependency exclusions is possible but compile-testing’s direct dependency complicates this. Upgrading compile-testing to versions 0.18–0.21, which likely remove tools.jar dependency and support newer JDKs, is the simplest solution to avoid errors while checking Java and dependency compatibility."}}
